115export function mountAiCoach() {
116 const chat = document.getElementById("aiChat");
117 const input = document.getElementById("aiInput");
118 const replies = [
119 "Berdasarkan data IMU-mu, kadens turun ke 162 spm di km terakhir — coba naikkan ke ~175 untuk efisiensi lebih baik.",
120 "Asimetri L/R-mu 8% kemarin. Tambahkan single-leg drill agar lebih seimbang.",
121 "HRV-mu naik 6ms — pemulihan bagus. Aman untuk sesi tempo hari ini.",
122 "Stabilitas anchor-mu meningkat 12% bulan ini. Pertahankan ritme pernapasan 4-detik.",
123 "Asupan protein pasca latihan masih kurang. Target 1.4 g/kg untuk pemulihan optimal.",
124 ];
125 let i = 0;
126 function send() {
127 const v = input.value.trim(); if (!v) return;
128 const me = document.createElement("div");
129 me.style.cssText = "align-self:flex-end;max-width:85%;background:var(--grad-brand);color:#fff;padding:10px 13px;border-radius:14px 14px 4px 14px;font-size:.82rem";
130 me.textContent = v; chat.appendChild(me); input.value = "";

2 NovaSport — Pembacaan Alat Wearable (Live Sensor Reading)

4 Membaca data sensor dari Smart-Enabled Equipment NovaSport.

6 DUA SUMBER DATA:
7 1. PERANGKAT NYATA — Web Bluetooth (GATT). Mendukung profil
8 standar Heart Rate (0x180D) & Battery (0x180F), plus service
9 kustom NovaSport untuk stream IMU 9-sumbu.
10 2. MODE SIMULASI — dipakai otomatis bila Web Bluetooth tidak
11 tersedia (Safari/Firefox/HTTP) atau pengguna membatalkan
12 dialog pairing. Demo Day tetap jalan tanpa hardware.

205/* ============================================================
206 MOUNT — koneksi, decoding, dan render loop
207 ============================================================ */
208export function mountWearableRead() {
209 const st = {
210 profile: "band",
211 rate: 25, // fps render
212 streaming: false,
213 recording: false,
214 source: null, // 'ble' | 'sim'
215 device: null, // BluetoothDevice
216 chars: [], // characteristic yang di-subscribe
217 timer: null,
218 t0: 0,
219 packets: 0,
220 dropped: 0,
221 hz: 0,
222 hzWindow: [],
223 battery: null,
224 hr: 0,
225 hrHist: [],
226 bias: { ax: 0, ay: 0, az: 0, gx: 0, gy: 0, gz: 0 },
227 hist: { ax: [], ay: [], az: [], gx: [], gy: [], gz: [] },
228 last: null,
229 orient: { roll: 0, pitch: 0, yaw: 0 },
230 samples: [], // buffer rekaman
231 phase: 0,
232 };
233 
234 /* ---------- Log konsol ---------- */
235 function log(msg, color = "#8fa4d8") {
236 const box = $("wrLog");
237 if (!box) return;
238 const line = document.createElement("div");
239 line.style.color = color;
240 line.textContent = `[${nowStr()}] ${msg}`;
241 box.appendChild(line);
242 while (box.children.length > 120) box.removeChild(box.firstChild);
243 box.scrollTop = box.scrollHeight;
244 }
245 
246 /* ---------- Kanal aktif per profil ---------- */
247 const CH_LABEL = { imu: "IMU 9-axis", hr: "Heart Rate", pressure: "Pressure", ir: "Infrared", emg: "EMG" };
248 function renderChannels() {
249 const box = $("wrChannels");
250 if (!box) return;
251 box.innerHTML = PROFILES[st.profile].ch
252 .map((c) => `<span class="chip chip-brand" style="font-size:.68rem">${CH_LABEL[c] || c}</span>`).join("");
253 }
254 
255 /* ---------- Web Bluetooth ---------- */
256 async function connect() {
257 if (!navigator.bluetooth) {
258 toast("Web Bluetooth tidak tersedia — memakai mode simulasi.", "warn");
259 log("navigator.bluetooth tidak ada → fallback simulasi", "#f7b733");
260 setSource("sim", PROFILES[st.profile].name + " (Simulasi)");
261 return;
262 }
263 try {
264 log("Meminta perangkat Bluetooth...", "#7ff0d2");
265 const device = await navigator.bluetooth.requestDevice({
266 filters: [
267 { namePrefix: PROFILES[st.profile].namePrefix },
268 { services: [HR_SERVICE] },
269 ],
270 optionalServices: [NS_IMU_SERVICE, HR_SERVICE, BAT_SERVICE],
271 });
272 st.device = device;
273 device.addEventListener("gattserverdisconnected", onDisconnected);
274 log(`Perangkat dipilih: ${device.name || device.id}`, "#7ff0d2");
275 
276 const server = await device.gatt.connect();
277 log("GATT server tersambung.", "#7ff0d2");
278 setSource("ble", device.name || "Perangkat BLE");
279 
280 await subscribeHeartRate(server);
281 await readBattery(server);
282 await subscribeImu(server);
283 
284 toast("Perangkat tersambung.", "ok");
285 if (!st.streaming) startStream();
286 } catch (err) {
287 if (err && err.name === "NotFoundError") {
288 log("Pairing dibatalkan → fallback simulasi", "#f7b733");
289 toast("Pairing dibatalkan — memakai mode simulasi.", "warn");
290 } else {
291 log("Gagal konek: " + (err && err.message ? err.message : err), "#ff7b8a");
292 toast("Gagal menghubungkan perangkat.", "warn");
293 }
294 setSource("sim", PROFILES[st.profile].name + " (Simulasi)");
295 }
296 }
297 
298 async function subscribeHeartRate(server) {
299 try {
300 const svc = await server.getPrimaryService(HR_SERVICE);
301 const ch = await svc.getCharacteristic(HR_CHAR);
302 await ch.startNotifications();
303 ch.addEventListener("characteristicvaluechanged", (e) => {
304 const bpm = decodeHeartRate(e.target.value);
305 if (bpm != null) { st.hr = bpm; pushHr(bpm); st.packets++; markPacket(); }
306 });
307 st.chars.push(ch);
308 log("Subscribe heart_rate_measurement (0x2A37) ✓", "#7ff0d2");
309 } catch { log("Service Heart Rate tidak tersedia di perangkat ini.", "#5b6b96"); }
310 }
311 
312 async function readBattery(server) {
313 try {
314 const svc = await server.getPrimaryService(BAT_SERVICE);
315 const ch = await svc.getCharacteristic(BAT_CHAR);
316 const v = await ch.readValue();
317 st.battery = v.getUint8(0);
318 log(`Baterai perangkat: ${st.battery}%`, "#7ff0d2");
319 try {
320 await ch.startNotifications();
321 ch.addEventListener("characteristicvaluechanged", (e) => { st.battery = e.target.value.getUint8(0); });
322 st.chars.push(ch);
323 } catch { /* notify baterai opsional */ }
324 } catch { log("Service Battery tidak tersedia.", "#5b6b96"); }
325 }
326 
327 async function subscribeImu(server) {
328 try {
329 const svc = await server.getPrimaryService(NS_IMU_SERVICE);
330 const ch = await svc.getCharacteristic(NS_IMU_CHAR);
331 await ch.startNotifications();
332 ch.addEventListener("characteristicvaluechanged", (e) => {
333 const f = decodeImuFrame(e.target.value);
334 if (f) { applyFrame(f); st.packets++; markPacket(); }
335 });
336 st.chars.push(ch);
337 log(`Subscribe NovaSport IMU stream ✓ (9-axis @ ${PROFILES[st.profile].hz} Hz)`, "#7ff0d2");
338 } catch { log("Service IMU NovaSport tidak ditemukan — hanya kanal standar.", "#5b6b96"); }
339 }
340 
341 /* ---------- Decoder ---------- */
342 
343 // Heart Rate Measurement (0x2A37): bit0 flags menentukan format nilai.
344 function decodeHeartRate(dv) {
345 if (!dv || dv.byteLength < 2) return null;
346 const flags = dv.getUint8(0);
347 return flags & 0x01 ? dv.getUint16(1, true) : dv.getUint8(1);
348 }
349 
350 // Frame IMU NovaSport: 9 × int16 little-endian + uint16 seq (20 byte).
351 // Skala: accel ±16g → /2048, gyro ±2000°/s → /16.4, mag µT → /10.
352 function decodeImuFrame(dv) {
353 if (!dv || dv.byteLength < 18) return null;
354 const i16 = (o) => dv.getInt16(o, true);
355 const f = {
356 ax: i16(0) / 2048, ay: i16(2) / 2048, az: i16(4) / 2048,
357 gx: i16(6) / 16.4, gy: i16(8) / 16.4, gz: i16(10) / 16.4,
358 mx: i16(12) / 10, my: i16(14) / 10, mz: i16(16) / 10,
359 seq: dv.byteLength >= 20 ? dv.getUint16(18, true) : null,
360 };
361 if (f.seq != null && st.last && st.last.seq != null) {
362 const gap = (f.seq - st.last.seq + 65536) % 65536;
363 if (gap > 1) st.dropped += gap - 1;
364 }
365 return f;
366 }
367 
368 function applyFrame(f) {
369 st.last = f;
370 // Orientasi dari akselerometer, dihaluskan complementary filter sederhana.
371 const ax = f.ax - st.bias.ax, ay = f.ay - st.bias.ay, az = f.az - st.bias.az;
372 const roll = Math.atan2(ay, az) * 57.2958;
373 const pitch = Math.atan2(-ax, Math.hypot(ay, az)) * 57.2958;
374 st.orient.roll = st.orient.roll * 0.85 + roll * 0.15;
375 st.orient.pitch = st.orient.pitch * 0.85 + pitch * 0.15;
376 st.orient.yaw = (st.orient.yaw + (f.gz - st.bias.gz) * 0.02 + 360) % 360;
377 if (st.recording) {
378 st.samples.push({ t: Date.now() - st.t0, ...f, hr: st.hr, roll: st.orient.roll, pitch: st.orient.pitch, yaw: st.orient.yaw });
379 }
380 }
381 
382 function pushHr(bpm) {
383 st.hrHist.push(bpm);
384 if (st.hrHist.length > 44) st.hrHist.shift();
385 }
386 
387 function markPacket() {
388 const now = performance.now();
389 st.hzWindow.push(now);
390 while (st.hzWindow.length && now - st.hzWindow[0] > 1000) st.hzWindow.shift();
391 st.hz = st.hzWindow.length;
392 }
393 
394 /* ---------- Generator simulasi ---------- */
395 // Model sinyal: napas lambat + tremor tangan + micro-drift giroskop,
396 // supaya kurva terlihat seperti pembacaan IMU sungguhan.
397 function simulateFrame() {
398 st.phase += 0.09;
399 const breath = Math.sin(st.phase * 0.35);
400 const tremor = Math.sin(st.phase * 6.1) * 0.06 + noise(0.05);
401 const sway = Math.sin(st.phase * 0.9) * 0.14;
402 // sesekali paket hilang, seperti BLE nyata
403 if (Math.random() < 0.004) { st.dropped++; return null; }
404 return {
405 ax: sway + tremor,
406 ay: breath * 0.18 + tremor * 0.7,
407 az: 1.0 + breath * 0.06 + noise(0.04),
408 gx: sway * 34 + noise(5),
409 gy: breath * 18 + noise(4),
410 gz: Math.sin(st.phase * 0.5) * 9 + noise(3),
411 mx: 22 + noise(1.5), my: -8 + noise(1.5), mz: 41 + noise(1.5),
412 seq: (st.packets + 1) % 65536,
413 };
414 }
415 
416 function simulateHr() {
417 const target = 96 + Math.sin(st.phase * 0.12) * 16;
418 st.hr = st.hr ? st.hr + (target - st.hr) * 0.12 + noise(1.6) : target;
419 st.hr = clamp(st.hr, 52, 195);
420 return Math.round(st.hr);
421 }
422 
423 /* ---------- Loop render ---------- */
424 function pushHist(k, v) {
425 const h = st.hist[k];
426 h.push(v);
427 if (h.length > 46) h.shift();
428 }
429 
430 function tick() {
431 // Mode simulasi menghasilkan frame sendiri; mode BLE hanya merender
432 // frame terakhir yang masuk lewat notifikasi GATT.
433 if (st.source !== "ble") {
434 const f = simulateFrame();
435 if (f) { applyFrame(f); st.packets++; markPacket(); }
436 pushHr(simulateHr());
437 if (st.battery == null) st.battery = 76;
438 }
439 render();
440 }
441 
442 function render() {
443 const f = st.last;
444 if ($("wrPackets")) $("wrPackets").textContent = st.packets.toLocaleString("id-ID");
445 if ($("wrHz")) $("wrHz").textContent = st.hz || 0;
446 if ($("wrBat")) $("wrBat").textContent = st.battery == null ? "—" : Math.round(st.battery);
447 if ($("wrDrop")) $("wrDrop").textContent = st.dropped;
448 if ($("wrLat")) $("wrLat").textContent = st.source === "ble" ? Math.round(8 + Math.random() * 6) + " ms" : Math.round(12 + Math.random() * 8) + " ms";
449 
450 if (f) {
451 const vals = {
452 ax: f.ax - st.bias.ax, ay: f.ay - st.bias.ay, az: f.az - st.bias.az,
453 gx: f.gx - st.bias.gx, gy: f.gy - st.bias.gy, gz: f.gz - st.bias.gz,
454 };
455 const colors = { ax: "#e23b4e", ay: "#12c4a6", az: "#3b65f2", gx: "#f59e0b", gy: "#7b62f7", gz: "#2596e8" };
456 for (const k of Object.keys(vals)) {
457 pushHist(k, vals[k]);
458 const sp = $(k + "-spark"), vv = $(k + "-val");
459 if (vv) vv.textContent = vals[k].toFixed(2);
460 if (sp && st.hist[k].length > 1) sp.innerHTML = sparkline(st.hist[k], colors[k], 150, 30);
461 }
462 const o = st.orient;
463 if ($("wrRoll")) $("wrRoll").textContent = Math.round(o.roll) + "°";
464 if ($("wrPitch")) $("wrPitch").textContent = Math.round(o.pitch) + "°";
465 if ($("wrYaw")) $("wrYaw").textContent = Math.round(o.yaw) + "°";
466 const cube = $("wrCube");
467 if (cube) cube.style.transform = `perspective(320px) rotateX(${clamp(o.pitch, -60, 60)}deg) rotateY(${clamp(o.roll, -60, 60)}deg) rotateZ(${(o.yaw * 0.2).toFixed(1)}deg)`;
468 
469 // log paket berkala supaya konsol tidak banjir
470 if (st.packets % 12 === 0) {
471 const fmt = (v) => (v >= 0 ? " " : "") + v.toFixed(2);
472 log(`seq=${String(f.seq ?? st.packets).padStart(5, "0")} a[${fmt(f.ax)}${fmt(f.ay)}${fmt(f.az)} ] g[${fmt(f.gx)}${fmt(f.gy)}${fmt(f.gz)} ] hr=${Math.round(st.hr)}`);
473 }
474 }
475 
476 // heart rate
477 const hr = Math.round(st.hr || 0);
478 const zone = hr < 100 ? 1 : hr < 130 ? 2 : hr < 155 ? 3 : hr < 172 ? 4 : 5;
479 const zc = ["#16a34a", "#12c4a6", "#f59e0b", "#f97316", "#e23b4e"][zone - 1];
480 if ($("wrHrZone")) { $("wrHrZone").textContent = hr ? hr + " bpm" : "— bpm"; $("wrHrZone").style.color = zc; }
481 if ($("wrHrRing")) $("wrHrRing").innerHTML = donut(clamp(Math.round(((hr - 50) / 140) * 100), 0, 100), { size: 132, color: zc, label: String(hr), sub: "bpm" });
482 if ($("wrHrSpark") && st.hrHist.length > 1) $("wrHrSpark").innerHTML = sparkline(st.hrHist, zc, 270, 38);
483 
484 // kualitas sinyal
485 const rssi = clamp(Math.round((st.source === "ble" ? 78 : 88) + noise(6)), 0, 100);
486 const integrity = st.packets ? clamp(Math.round(100 - (st.dropped / st.packets) * 100), 0, 100) : 0;
487 const calib = clamp(Math.round(94 - Math.abs(st.bias.ax + st.bias.ay) * 20 + noise(3)), 0, 100);
488 if ($("wrQuality")) $("wrQuality").innerHTML =
489 gaugeRow("Kekuatan Sinyal (RSSI)", rssi, "#12c4a6") +
490 gaugeRow("Integritas Paket", integrity, "#3b65f2") +
491 gaugeRow("Stabilitas Kalibrasi", calib, "#f59e0b");
492 
493 // durasi & jumlah sampel rekaman
494 if (st.recording) {
495 const s = Math.floor((Date.now() - st.t0) / 1000);
496 if ($("wrDur")) $("wrDur").textContent = String(Math.floor(s / 60)).padStart(2, "0") + ":" + String(s % 60).padStart(2, "0");
497 if ($("wrSamples")) $("wrSamples").textContent = st.samples.length.toLocaleString("id-ID");
498 }
499 }
500 
501 /* ---------- Kontrol ---------- */
502 function setSource(src, label) {
503 st.source = src;
504 const badge = $("wrSrc");
505 if (badge) {
506 badge.textContent = src === "ble" ? "Bluetooth" : "Simulasi";
507 badge.className = "pill " + (src === "ble" ? "ok" : "info");
508 }
509 if ($("wrDevName")) $("wrDevName").textContent = label;
510 if ($("wrState")) {
511 $("wrState").className = "pill " + (src === "ble" ? "ok" : "warn");
512 $("wrState").innerHTML = src === "ble" ? "● Tersambung" : "● Simulasi";
513 }
514 }
515 
516 function startStream() {
517 if (st.streaming) return pauseStream();
518 if (!st.source) setSource("sim", PROFILES[st.profile].name + " (Simulasi)");
519 st.streaming = true;
520 st.hzWindow = [];
521 if ($("wrStream")) $("wrStream").innerHTML = "⏸ Jeda Pembacaan";
522 if ($("wrImuState")) { $("wrImuState").className = "pill ok"; $("wrImuState").textContent = "● Streaming"; }
523 clearInterval(st.timer);
524 st.timer = setInterval(tick, 1000 / st.rate);
525 log(`Pembacaan dimulai — ${PROFILES[st.profile].name} @ ${st.rate} fps render`, "#7ff0d2");
526 }
527 
528 function pauseStream() {
529 st.streaming = false;
530 clearInterval(st.timer);
531 st.timer = null;
532 if ($("wrStream")) $("wrStream").innerHTML = "▶ Lanjut Pembacaan";
533 if ($("wrImuState")) { $("wrImuState").className = "pill warn"; $("wrImuState").textContent = "⏸ Jeda"; }
534 log("Pembacaan dijeda.", "#f7b733");
535 }
536 
537 function toggleRecord() {
538 st.recording = !st.recording;
539 const b = $("wrRecord");
540 if (st.recording) {
541 st.samples = [];
542 st.t0 = Date.now();
543 if (b) { b.innerHTML = "⏹ Hentikan Rekam"; b.className = "btn btn-primary btn-block"; }
544 if (!st.streaming) startStream();
545 toast("Merekam sesi sensor...", "ok");
546 log("● REC — perekaman sampel dimulai", "#ff7b8a");
547 } else {
548 if (b) { b.innerHTML = "⏺ Rekam Sesi"; b.className = "btn btn-outline btn-block"; }
549 toast(`Rekaman selesai — ${st.samples.length} sampel.`, "ok");
550 log(`■ STOP — ${st.samples.length} sampel tersimpan di buffer`, "#7ff0d2");
551 }
552 }
553 
554 function calibrate() {
555 if (!st.last) return toast("Mulai pembacaan dulu sebelum kalibrasi.", "warn");
556 st.bias = { ax: st.last.ax, ay: st.last.ay, az: st.last.az - 1, gx: st.last.gx, gy: st.last.gy, gz: st.last.gz };
557 st.orient = { roll: 0, pitch: 0, yaw: 0 };
558 Object.keys(st.hist).forEach((k) => (st.hist[k] = []));
559 toast("Kalibrasi nol tersimpan.", "ok");
560 log("Kalibrasi nol: bias sensor diperbarui ✓", "#7ff0d2");
561 }
562 
563 function download(name, text, type) {
564 const url = URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([text], { type }));
565 const a = document.createElement("a");
566 a.href = url;
567 a.download = name;
568 a.click();
569 setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(url), 1000);
570 }
571 
572 function exportCsv() {
573 if (!st.samples.length) return toast("Belum ada sampel — rekam sesi dulu.", "warn");
574 const cols = ["t", "ax", "ay", "az", "gx", "gy", "gz", "mx", "my", "mz", "roll", "pitch", "yaw", "hr"];
575 const rows = st.samples.map((s) => cols.map((c) => (typeof s[c] === "number" ? s[c].toFixed(4) : s[c] ?? "")).join(","));
576 download(`novasport-${st.profile}-${Date.now()}.csv`, cols.join(",") + "\n" + rows.join("\n"), "text/csv");
577 toast("CSV diunduh.", "ok");
578 }
579 
580 function exportJson() {
581 if (!st.samples.length) return toast("Belum ada sampel — rekam sesi dulu.", "warn");
582 download(`novasport-${st.profile}-${Date.now()}.json`, JSON.stringify({
583 device: PROFILES[st.profile].name,
584 profile: st.profile,
585 source: st.source,
586 samplingHz: PROFILES[st.profile].hz,
587 recordedAt: new Date(st.t0).toISOString(),
588 packets: st.packets,
589 dropped: st.dropped,
590 samples: st.samples,
591 }, null, 2), "application/json");
592 toast("JSON diunduh.", "ok");
593 }
594 
595 function onDisconnected() {
596 log("Perangkat terputus.", "#ff7b8a");
597 toast("Perangkat terputus.", "warn");
598 st.device = null;
599 st.chars = [];
600 setSource("sim", PROFILES[st.profile].name + " (Simulasi)");
601 }
602 
603 function disconnect() {
604 if (st.device && st.device.gatt.connected) st.device.gatt.disconnect();
605 st.device = null;
606 st.chars = [];
607 pauseStream();
608 st.source = null;
609 if ($("wrSrc")) { $("wrSrc").textContent = "—"; $("wrSrc").className = "pill muted"; }
610 if ($("wrState")) { $("wrState").className = "pill danger"; $("wrState").innerHTML = "● Terputus"; }
611 if ($("wrDevName")) $("wrDevName").textContent = "—";
612 log("Koneksi diputus oleh pengguna.", "#f7b733");
613 }
614 
615 /* ---------- Wiring ---------- */
616 renderChannels();
617 
618 $("wrConnect")?.addEventListener("click", connect);
619 $("wrStream")?.addEventListener("click", startStream);
620 $("wrRecord")?.addEventListener("click", toggleRecord);
621 $("wrDisconnect")?.addEventListener("click", disconnect);
622 $("wrCal")?.addEventListener("click", calibrate);
623 $("wrCsv")?.addEventListener("click", exportCsv);
624 $("wrJson")?.addEventListener("click", exportJson);
625 $("wrClearLog")?.addEventListener("click", () => { $("wrLog").innerHTML = ""; });
626 
627 $("wrProfile")?.addEventListener("change", (e) => {
628 st.profile = e.target.value;
629 renderChannels();
630 if (st.source !== "ble") setSource("sim", PROFILES[st.profile].name + " (Simulasi)");
631 log(`Profil perangkat: ${PROFILES[st.profile].name} (${PROFILES[st.profile].hz} Hz)`, "#7ff0d2");
632 });
633 
634 $("wrRate")?.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
635 const b = e.target.closest("button[data-rate]");
636 if (!b) return;
637 $("wrRate").querySelectorAll("button").forEach((x) => x.classList.remove("active"));
638 b.classList.add("active");
639 st.rate = +b.dataset.rate;
640 if (st.streaming) { clearInterval(st.timer); st.timer = setInterval(tick, 1000 / st.rate); }
641 });
642 
643 // Auto-start dalam mode simulasi supaya halaman tidak terlihat mati.
644 setSource("sim", PROFILES[st.profile].name + " (Simulasi)");
645 startStream();
646 
647 /* ---------- Cleanup saat pindah route ---------- */
648 return () => {
649 clearInterval(st.timer);
650 st.chars.forEach((c) => { try { c.stopNotifications(); } catch { /* perangkat sudah lepas */ } });
651 if (st.device && st.device.gatt.connected) { try { st.device.gatt.disconnect(); } catch { /* noop */ } }
652 };
653}
